Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > General
Content: The prose-discovery-message element is the root element of this XML document and it can be one of the following elements: - DISCOVERY_REQUEST ; - DISCOVERY_RESPONSE ; MATCH_REPORT ; MATCH_REPORT_ACK ; UE_REGISTRATION_REQUEST ; UE_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE ; APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_REQUEST ; APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE ; PROXIMITY_REQUEST ; PROXIMITY_REQUEST_RESPONSE ; PROXIMITY_ALERT ; UE_DEREGISTRATION_REQUEST ; UE_DEREGISTRATION_RESPONSE ; CANCEL_PROXIMITY_REQUEST ; CANCEL_PROXIMITY_RESPONSE ; PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION ; PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION_RESPONSE ; - DISCOVERY_UPDATE_REQUEST ; - DISCOVERY_UPDATE_RESPONSE - ANNOUNCE_ALERT_REQUEST ; - ANNOUNCE_ALERT_RESPONSE ; message-ext element containing other discovery message defined in future releases; or an element from other namespaces defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of DISCOVERY_REQUEST
Content: The DISCOVERY_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: zero, one or more discovery-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as announcing or monitoring requests for open ProSe direct discovery . Each discovery-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a command element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.2; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.3; a Prose-Application-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.4; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.5; a Discovery-Entry-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; an optional Requested-Timer element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.27; an optional metadata element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.21; an optional Announcing-PLMN-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.64; zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one, or more restricted-discovery-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as announcing or monitoring requests for restricted ProSe directed discovery model A or transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as discoveree or discoverer requests for restricted ProSe directed discovery model B. Each restricted-discovery-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a command element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.2; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.3; a RPAUID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.30; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.5; f) a discovery-type element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.25; zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38; an announcing-type element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.31; an application-level-container element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.32; zero or one discovery-model element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.41; zero or one Announcing-PLMN-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.64; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; an optional Requested-Timer element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.27; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; an optional "network-initiated transaction method" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.63; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of DISCOVERY_RESPONSE
Content: The DISCOVERY_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: a Current-Time element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.23; a Max-Offset element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.24; zero, one or more response-announce element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to an announcing request if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each response-announce consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; zero, one or more ProSe-Application-Code elements containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.6;c) zero or one ProSe Application Code-ACE element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.70; zero, or one validity-timer-T4000 element containing the parameter defined in 12.2.2.7; zero, or, one discovery-key element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.9; a Discovery-Entry-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-monitor element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a monitoring request if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each response-monitor consists of: [a)]a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; [b)]zero, one or more discovery-filter elements containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.12; [c)]a Discovery-Entry-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; [d)]zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38; [da)]zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; [e)]zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; [f)]zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and [g)]zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-announce-response element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to an announcing request for restricted ProSe direct discovery model A if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each restricted-announce-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; zero or one ProSe-Restricted-Code element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.34; zero, one or more ProSe-Restricted-Code-Suffix-Range element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.35; zero or one validity-timer-T4007 element containing the parameter defined in 12.2.2.39; zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38; zero or one restricted-code-security-material element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.40; an optional on-demand-announcing-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.36; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-monitor-response element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a monitoring request for restricted ProSe direct discovery model A if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each restricted-monitor-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; zero, one or more restricted-discovery-filter elements containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.37; zero or one ACE-enabled-indicator element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.38 a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; an application-level-container element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.32; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-discoveree-response element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a discoveree UE's request for restricted ProSe direct discovery model B if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each restricted-discoveree-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a ProSe-Response-Code element containing the element defined in subclause12.2.2.42; one or more query-filter elements containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.43; a validity-timer-T4011 element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.44; zero or one restricted-code-security-material element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.40; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-discoverer-response element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a discoverer UE's request for restricted ProSe direct discovery model B if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each restricted-discoverer-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; one or more subquery-result elements containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.45; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to an announcing or monitoring requests if the ProSe Function cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a PC3-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.8. zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; an optional "network-initiated transaction method " attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.63; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of MATCH_REPORT
Content: The MATCH_REPORT element contains one or more of the following element: zero, one or more match-report element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function to report a matching of the direct discovery. Each match-report consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a ProSe-Application-Code element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.6; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.3; a Monitored-PLMN-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.16; an optional VPLMN-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.17; a MIC element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.11; a UTC-based-counter element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.18; a metadata-flag element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.20; a MessageType element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.10; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-match element which contain transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function to report a matching of the restricted direct discovery model A or model B. Each restricted-match consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause subclause 12.2.2.3; a discovery-type element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.25 an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.5 an RPAUID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.30; a Restricted-Code-Discovered element containing the ProSe Restricted Code parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.34 or ProSe Response Code parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.42; an optional MIC element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.11; an optional MessageType element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.10; an optional UTC-based-counter element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.18; a metadata-flag element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.20; zero or one PC5-tech element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.71; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of MATCH_REPORT_ACK
Content: The MATCH_REPORT_ACK element contains one or more of the following elements: a Current-Time element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.23; zero, one or more match-ack element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a match report if the ProSe Function accepts the report. Each match-ack consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a ProSe-Application-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.4; a validity-timer-T4004 element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.19; an optional metadata element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.21; an optional metadata-index-mask element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.62; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; a mandatory "match-report-refresh-timer-T4006" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.26; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more restricted-match-ack element which contain transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a match report if the ProSe Function accepts the report. Each restricted-match-ack consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.5; an RPAUID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.30; a validity-timer-T4016 element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.60; an optional metadata element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.21; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; an optional "match-report-refresh-timer-T4017" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.61; and [ i) ] zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more match-reject element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to a match report if the ProSe Function cannot accept the match report. Each match-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.1; a PC3-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.8; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of UE_REGISTRATION_REQUEST
Content: The UE_REGISTRATION_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more UE-register-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function to register the UE. Each UE-register-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.2; a WLAN-link-layer-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.6; a method-for-server-initiated-transaction element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.14; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of UE_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE
Content: The UE_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: zero, one or more response-register element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the UE_REGISTRATION_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each response-register consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an EPC-ProSe-User-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.7; a server-initiated-method-config element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.15; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the UE_REGISTRATION_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; a PC3-EPC-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.5; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_REQUEST
Content: The APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more application-register-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function to activate EPC-level ProSe discovery for a specific application. Each application-register-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an EPC-ProSe-User-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.7. an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.3; an Application-Layer-User-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE
Content: The APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: zero, one or more response-register element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each response-register consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; one or more allowed-range-class element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.8; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero,one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the APPLICATION_REGISTRATION_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; a PC3-EPC-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.5; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of PROXIMITY_REQUEST
Content: The PROXIMITY_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more proximity-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function to request to be alerted when it enters in proximity with a targeted UE. Each proximity-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an EPC-ProSe-User-ID-A element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.7; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.3; an Application-Layer-User-ID-A element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; an Application-Layer-User-ID-B element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; a requested-range-class element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.8; a UE-A-Location element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.11; a time-window element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.9; an optional WLAN-indication element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.12; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of PROXIMITY_REQUEST_RESPONSE
Content: The PROXIMITY_REQUEST_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: zero,one or more response-accept element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the PROXIMITY_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function accepts the request. Each response-register consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as a response to the PROXIMITY_REQUEST message if the ProSe Function cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; [ a) ] a PC3-EPC-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.5; [ a) ] zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of PROXIMITY_ALERT
Content: The PROXIMITY_ALERT element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more proximity-alert element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the (UE A) and optionally to targeted (UE B) to alert them that they have entered in proximity. Each proximity-alert consists of: [ a) ] transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.3; an Application-Layer-User-ID-A element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; an Application-Layer-User-ID-B element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; an optional assistance-information element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.13; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of UE_DEREGISTRATION_REQUEST
Content: The UE_DEREGISTRATION_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more UE-deregister-request element which contains transactions sent either from the UE to the ProSe Function or from the ProSe Function to the UE to deregister the UE. Each UE-deregister-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an EPC-ProSe-User-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.7; a PC3-EPC-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.5; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of UE_DEREGISTRATION_RESPONSE
Content: The UE_DEREGISTRATION_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more UE-deregister-response element which contains transactions sent either from the UE to the ProSe Function or from the ProSe Function to the UE to complete the UE deregistration. Each UE-deregister-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of CANCEL_PROXIMITY_REQUEST
Content: The CANCEL_PROXIMITY_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more cancel-proximity-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function or from the ProSe Function to the UE to request cancellation of an ongoing proximity request. Each cancel_proximity-request consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an EPC-ProSe-User-ID-A element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.7; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.3; an Application-Layer-User-ID-B element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.4; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of CANCEL_PROXIMITY_RESPONSE
Content: The CANCEL_PROXIMITY_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more cancel-proximity-responset element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE or from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response to CANCEL_PROXIMITY_REQUEST message. Each cancel_proximity-response consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION
Content: The PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more proximity-request-validation element which contains transactions sent by the ProSe Function to the targeted UE (UE B) to request confirmation of permission for proximity request. Each proximity-request-validation consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; an application-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.3; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION_RESPONSE
Content: The PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: zero, one or more response-accept element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response to the PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION message if the application in the UE accepts the request. Each response-accept consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response to the PROXIMITY_REQUEST_VALIDATION message if the application in the UE does not accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.1; [ a) ] a PC3-EPC-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.3.2.5. [ a) ] zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of DISCOVERY_UPDATE_REQUEST
Content: The DISCOVERY_UPDATE_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more discovery-update-request element which contains transactions sent from the ProSe Function to the UE as announcing or monitoring requests. Each discovery-update-request consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.3; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; an optional update-info element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.29; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of DISCOVERY_UPDATE_RESPONSE
Content: The DISCOVERY_UPDATE _RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: one or more response-update element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response if the UE accepts the request. Each discovery-update-response consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; a discovery-entry-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response if the UE cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; a PC3-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.8. zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of ANNOUNCING_ALERT_REQUEST
Content: The ANNOUNCING_ALERT_REQUEST element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more announcing-alert-request element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as announcing or monitoring requests. Each announcing-alert-request consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.3; a RPAUID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.30; a Discovery-Entry-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.33; a ProSe-Restricted-Code element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.34; zero, one or more ProSe-Restricted-Code-Suffix-Range element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.35; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > Semantics > Semantics of ANNOUNCING_ALERT_RESPONSE
Content: The ANNOUNCING_ALERT_RESPONSE element contains one or more of the following elements: One or more announcing-alert-response element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as announcing or monitoring requests. Each announcing-alert-response consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero, one or more response-reject element which contains transactions sent from the UE to the ProSe Function as a response if the UE cannot accept the request. Each response-reject consists of: [ a) ] a prose-function-transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.28; a PC3-control-protocol-cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.2.2.8. zero, one or more elements defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > ProSe discovery messages > PC5_DISCOVERY > Message definition
Content: This message is sent by the UE over the PC5 interface for open ProSe direct discovery and restricted ProSe direct discovery. See table 11.2.5.1.1, table 11.2.5.1.1A, table 11.2.5.1.2, table 11.2.5.1.3, table 11.2.5.1.4, table 11.2.5.1.5, table 11.2.5.1.6, table 11.2.5.1.7, table 11.2.5.1.8, table 11.2.5.1.9 and table 11.2.5.1.10.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > Messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface > General
Content: This subclause defines XML schema and MIME type related to messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > Messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface > application/3gpp-prose-pc3ch+xml
Content: The MIME type is used to carry information related to message transmitted over the PC3ch interface. It shall be coded as an XML document compliant to the XML schema in subclause 11.3.3 containing one of the following messages: - USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST; or - USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST_RESPONSE. Each of those messages is presented in the XML document as an XML element named after the corresponding message. The XML document can contain elements and attributes defined in future releases of this document. Receiving entity ignores any element not defined by this version of the specification and any attribute not defined by this version of the specification.

Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > Messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface > Semantics > General
Content: The prose-pc3ch-message element is the root element of this XML document. The prose-pc3ch-message element contains one of the following: [ a) ] USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST element; USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST_RESPONSE element; anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; or one or more elements from other namespace defined in future releases. The prose-pc3ch-message element contains zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > Messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface > Semantics > Semantics of USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST
Content: The USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST element contains: [ a) ] a transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.1; a UE-identity element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.2; one or more usage-information-report elements; zero, one or more app-specific-info element; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The usage-information-report element carries one usage information report. The usage-information-report element contains: [ a) ] mandatory "sequence-number" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.3; zero, one or more coverage elements; zero, one or more group element; zero, one or more radio-parameter-set elements; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The coverage element carries information whether the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage or out of E-UTRAN coverage. The coverage element contains: [ a) ] mandatory "in-coverage" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.4; optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time when the information given in the element start being valid; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage, zero, one or more location elements; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The location element carries information about an E-UTRAN cell where the UE was camping on or which the UE used in the EMM-CONNECTED mode. The location element contains: [ a) ] an optional "ECGI" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.5; an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time when the information given in the element start being valid; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The radio-parameter-set element carries information about the configured radio parameters for the ProSe direct communication applicable in the geographical area of the UE. The radio-parameter-set element contains: [ a) ] a mandatory "params" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.6; a mandatory "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time when the information given in the element start being valid; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The group element carries information about a ProSe group. The group element contains: [ a) ] a mandatory "prose-layer2-group-ID" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.9; a mandatory "prose-group-IP-multicast-address" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.10; an optional "first-transmission-timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first transmission to the ProSe Group IP multicast address in the collection period; an optional "first-reception-timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first reception from the ProSe Group IP multicast address in the collection period; a UE-source-IP-address element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.11, of the UE; a prose-UE-id element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.12, of the UE; zero, one or more transmitter element; zero, one or more transmission element; zero, one or more reception element; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The transmitter element carries information about a transmitter in a ProSe group. The transmitter element contains: [ a) ] a mandatory "source-IP-address" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.11, of the transmitter; a mandatory "prose-UE-id" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.12, of the transmitter; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The transmission element carries information about a transmission in a ProSe group. The transmission element contains: [ a) ] a mandatory "in-coverage" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.4; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage when transmitting the data, an optional "ECGI" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.5, indicating E-UTRAN Cell Global Identification of the E-UTRAN cell where the UE was camping on or which the UE used in the EMM-CONNECTED mode when transmitting the data; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage and the "ECGI" attribute is included: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets transmitted to the ProSe group: when the UE was camping on a cell identified by the "ECGI" attribute when transmitting the data; or when the UE used in the EMM-CONNECTED mode a cell identified by the "ECGI" attribute when transmitting the data; and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first transmission in the E-UTRAN cell; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage and the "ECGI" attribute is not included: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets transmitted to the ProSe group during the in E-UTRAN coverage period: and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first transmission during the in E-UTRAN coverage period; if the UE was out of E-UTRAN coverage: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets transmitted to the ProSe group during the out of E-UTRAN coverage period; and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first transmission during the out of E-UTRAN coverage period; an optional "radio-resources-ind" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.14; an optional "radio-frequency" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.15; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The reception element carries information about a reception in a ProSe group. The reception element contains: [ a) ] a mandatory "in-coverage" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.4 indicating whether the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage when receiving the data; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage when receiving the data, an optional "ECGI" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.5 indicating E-UTRAN Cell Global Identification of the E-UTRAN cell where the UE was camping on or which the UE used in the EMM-CONNECTED mode when receiving the data; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage and the "ECGI" attribute is included: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets received from the ProSe group: when the UE was camping on a cell identified by the "ECGI" attribute when receiving the data; or when the UE used in the EMM-CONNECTED mode a cell identified by the "ECGI" attribute when receiving the data; and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first reception in the E-UTRAN cell; if the UE was in E-UTRAN coverage and the "ECGI" attribute is not included: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets received from the ProSe group during the in E-UTRAN coverage period: and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first reception during the in E-UTRAN coverage period; if the UE was out of E-UTRAN coverage: a mandatory "amount" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.13 indicating the amount of octets received from the ProSe group during the out of E-UTRAN coverage period; and an optional "timestamp" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.8 indicating date and time of the first reception during the out of E-UTRAN coverage period; an optional "radio-resources-ind" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.14; an optional "radio-frequency" attribute containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.15; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The app-specific-info element contains an application specific data received from upper layers during the collection period.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > Messages transmitted over the PC3ch interface > Semantics > Semantics of USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST_RESPONSE
Content: The USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST_RESPONSE element contains: [ a) ] one of response-accept element and response-reject element; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The response-accept element indicates that a related USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST message was accepted . The response-accept element contains: [ a) ] transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.1 indicating the value of the transaction ID of the related USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_ LIST message; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases. The response-reject element indicates that a related USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_LIST message was rejected . The response-reject element contains: [ a) ] transaction-ID element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.1 indicating the value of the transaction ID of the related USAGE_INFORMATION_REPORT_ LIST message; cause-value element containing the parameter defined in subclause 12.4.2.7; zero or one anyExt element containing elements defined in future releases; zero, one or more elements from other namespaces defined in future releases; and zero, one or more attributes defined in future releases.
Title Path: Message functional definitions and contents > PC5 Signalling messages > Overview
Content: This clause defines the structure of the PC5 Signalling messages exchanged between two ProSe-enabled UEs over the PC5 interface. These are standard L3 messages as defined in 3GPP TS 24.007 . Each definition given in the present clause includes: a) a table listing the Information Elements (IE) present in the message and the order of their appearance in the message. All IEs that may be repeated are explicitly indicated (The V, LV and LV-E formatted IEs, which contain the imperative part of the message, occur before the T, TV, TLV and TLV-E formatted IEs which contain the non-imperative part of the message, see 3GPP TS 24.007 ). In a (maximal) sequence of consecutive IEs with half octet length, the first IE with half octet length occupies bits 1 to 4 of octet N, the second IE bits 5 to 8 of octet N, the third IE bits 1 to 4 of octet N+1 etc. Such a sequence always has an even number of elements. For each information element the table indicates: 1. The Information Element Identifier (IEI), in hexadecimal notation, if the IE has format T, TV, TLV or TLV E. If the IEI has half octet length, it is specified by a notation representing the IEI as a hexadecimal digit followed by a "-" (example: B-). NOTE: The same IEI can be used for different information element types in different messages of the same protocol. The name of the information element (which is indicative of the semantics of the element). The name of the information element followed by "IE" or "information element" is used as reference to the information element within a message. The name of the type of the information element (which indicates the coding of the value part of the IE), and generally, the referenced subclause of clause 12 of the present document describing the value part of the information element. The presence requirement indication (M, C, or O) for the IE as defined in 3GPP TS 24.007 . The format of the information element (T, V, TV, LV, TLV, LV-E or TLV-E) as defined in 3GPP TS 24.007 . The length of the information element (or permissible range of lengths), in octets, in the message, where "?" means that the maximum length of the IE is only constrained by link layer protocol. This indication is non-normative. b) subclauses specifying, where appropriate, conditions for IEs with presence requirement C or O in the relevant message which together with other conditions specified in the present document define when the information elements shall be included or not, what non-presence of such IEs means, and for IEs with presence requirement C the static conditions for presence or non-presence of the IEs or for both cases (see 3GPP TS 24.007 ).
